MA	<p>bb2c_0bb3</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>SrfI</i></span> is derived from Streptomyces species Srf, and <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>AgeI</i></span> is derived from Agrobacterium gelatinovorum.
